mysql> SELECT
    ->     year,
    ->     SUM(new_cases) AS total_cases,
    ->     SUM(new_deaths) AS total_deaths
    -> FROM covid_analytics_cleaned
    -> WHERE country = 'India'
    -> GROUP BY year
    -> ORDER BY year;

mysql> SELECT
    ->     year,
    ->     month,
    ->     month_name,
    ->     SUM(new_cases) AS monthly_cases,
    ->     LAG(SUM(new_cases)) OVER (
    ->         ORDER BY year, month
    ->     ) AS previous_month_cases
    -> FROM covid_analytics_cleaned
    -> WHERE country = 'India'
    -> GROUP BY year, month, month_name
    -> ORDER BY year, month;
